An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

stderr | src/agent/qa/chartsOutcome/widgetActions.outcome.test.jsx > a widget's own Refresh, through its door (C3-112 Scanner, C3-208 Period Sort) > C3-208 held-out "refresh the period sort": the widget's own data hook re-reads once; the receipt follows it; nothing else changes
An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

stderr | src/agent/qa/chartsOutcome/widgetActions.outcome.test.jsx > a widget's own Refresh, through its door (C3-112 Scanner, C3-208 Period Sort) > two Scanner widgets: the request tells them apart by position, and "the right scanner" refreshes that one only
An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

stderr | src/agent/qa/chartsOutcome/widgetActions.outcome.test.jsx > the Scanner back to its picker (C3-108), as its own "change scan" writes it > held-out "leave this scan in the scanner": scanKey and scanName are cleared and the picker shows; Undo puts the scan back exactly
An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

stderr | src/agent/qa/chartsOutcome/widgetActions.outcome.test.jsx > the Scanner back to its picker (C3-108), as its own "change scan" writes it > held-out "take the scanner back to its picker": scanKey and scanName are cleared and the picker shows; Undo puts the scan back exactly
An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act

stderr | src/agent/qa/chartsOutcome/watchlistLook.outcome.test.jsx > C3-103: the list's appearance, through the ⚙ panel's own writer (this widget's opts.settings) > held-out "hide the logos in my watchlist": asked which first (nothing written), then the picked widget reads it back; the other Watchlist widget is untouched; Undo restores the opts exactly
An update to Live inside a test was not wrapped in act(...).

When testing, code that causes React state updates should be wrapped into act(...):

act(() => {
  /* fire events that update state */
});
/* assert on the output */

This ensures that you're testing the behavior the user would see in the browser. Learn more at https://react.dev/link/wrap-tests-with-act
